L[i, j] = abs(Vi)**2 * np.real(Yij) + np.real(Vi * np.conj(Yij) * Vj)\n\n                    M[i, i] += -abs(Vj)**2 * np.imag(Yij)\n                    L[i, i] += abs(Vj)**2 * np.real(Yij)\n\n        J[:n, :n] = H\n        J[:n, n:] = N\n        J[n:, :n] = M\n        J[n:, n:] = L\n\n        return J\n\n    def extract_results(self, V, Y, bus_data, gen_data, cost_data, ref_bus):\n        \"\"\"Extract results from solved power flow\"\"\"\n        results = {\n            'summary': {},\n            'generators': [],\n            'buses': [],\n            'most_loaded_branches': [],\n            'feasibility_check': {}\n        }\n\n        total_load_MW = sum(b['pd'] for b in bus_data.values())\n        total_load_MVAr = sum(b['qd'] for b in bus_data.values())\n\n        total_gen_MW = 0\n        total_gen_MVAr = 0\n\n        total_cost = 0\n\n        bus_voltages = {}\n        for bus_id, data in bus_data.items():\n            idx = bus_id - 1\n            vm = abs(V[idx])\n            va = np.angle(V[idx]) * 180 / math.pi\n\n            bus_voltages[bus_id] = {'vm': vm, 'va': va}\n\n            results['buses'].append({\n                'id': bus_id,\n                'vm_pu': vm,\n                'va_deg': va,\n                'vmin_pu': data['vmin'],\n                'vmax_pu': data['vmax']\n            })\n\n        for bus_id, gens in gen_data.items():\n            for gen in gens:\n                idx = gen['id']\n                bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n                vm = abs(V[bus_idx])\n\n                Pg = gen['pg']\n                Qg = gen['qg']\n\n                if idx in cost_data:\n                    c2 = cost_data[idx]['c2']\n                    c1 = cost_data[idx]['c1']\n                    c0 = cost_data[idx]['c0']\n                    total_cost += c2 * Pg**2 + c1 * Pg + c0\n\n                total_gen_MW += Pg\n                total_gen_MVAr += Qg\n\n                results['generators'].append({\n                    'id': idx + 1,\n                    'bus': bus_id,\n                    'pg_MW': Pg,\n                    'qg_MVAr': Qg,\n                    'pmin_MW': gen['pmin'],\n                    'pmax_MW': gen['pmax'],\n                    'qmin_MVAr': gen['qmin'],\n                    'qmax_MVAr': gen['qmax']\n                })\n\n        branch_flows = []\n        for i, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            fbus = int(branch[0]) - 1\n            tbus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            rateA = branch[5] if branch[5] > 0 else float('inf')\n\n            If = Y[fbus, fbus] * V[fbus] + Y[fbus, tbus] * V[tbus]\n            It = Y[tbus, fbus] * V[tbus] + Y[tbus, tbus] * V[tbus]\n\n            Sf = V[fbus] * np.conj(If)\n            St = V[tbus] * np.conj(It)\n\n            flow_from_MVA = abs(Sf) * self.baseMVA\n            flow_to_MVA = abs(St) * self.baseMVA\n\n            loading = 0\n            if rateA != float('inf'):\n                loading = max(flow_from_MVA, flow_to_MVA) / rateA * 100\n\n            branch_flows.append({\n                'from_bus': int(branch[0]),\n                'to_bus': int(branch[1]),\n                'loading_pct': loading,\n                'flow_from_MVA': flow_from_MVA * self.baseMVA,\n                'flow_to_MVA': flow_to_MVA * self.baseMVA,\n                'limit_MVA': rateA * self.baseMVA if rateA != float('inf') else 0\n            })\n\n        branch_flows.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n        results['most_loaded_branches'] = branch_flows[:10]\n\n        total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n        results['summary'] = {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': total_cost,\n            'total_load_MW': total_load_MW,\n            'total_load_MVAr': total_load_MVAr,\n            'total_generation_MW': total_gen_MW,\n            'total_generation_MVAr': total_gen_MVAr,\n            'total_losses_MW': total_losses_MW,\n            'solver_status': 'optimal'\n        }\n\n        max_v_violation = 0\n        for bus in results['buses']:\n            if bus['vm_pu'] < bus['vmin_pu']:\n                max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus['vmin_pu'] - bus['vm_pu'])\n            elif bus['vm_pu'] > bus['vmax_pu']:\n                max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus['vm_pu'] - bus['vmax_pu'])\n\n        max_branch_overload = max([b['loading_pct'] for b in branch_flows]) if branch_flows else 0\n\n        results['feasibility_check'] = {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.002,\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': max_v_violation,\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': max(0, max_branch_overload - 100)\n        }\n\n        return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    solver = ACOPFSolver('network.json')\n    results = solver.solve()\n\n    with open('report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n=== AC-OPF Solution Summary ===\")\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${results['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {results['summary']['total_load_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {results['summary']['total_generation_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: {results['summary']['total_losses_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"\\nSolver V, Y, bus_data, P_gen, Q_gen):\n        \"\"\"Build power flow Jacobian\"\"\"\n        n = len(V)\n        J = np.zeros((2*n, 2*n))\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            for j in range(n):\n solve_power_flow(self, Y, bus_data, gen_data, ref_bus, P_gen_init, Q_gen_init):\n        \"\"\"Solve power flow with fixed generation\"\"\"\n        V = np.ones(self.n_bus, dtype=complex)\n Y[fbus, fbus] += (y + Y_shunt) / (abs(a)**2)\n                Y[tbus, tbus] += (y + Y_shunt)\n                Y[fbus, tbus] -= y / a\n                Y[tbus, fbus] -= y / np.conj(a)\n solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve AC-OPF\"\"\"\n        Y = self.build_Y_matrix()\n\n        bus_data = {}\n        for b in self.buses:\n            bus_id = int(b[0])\n            bus_data[bus_id] = {\n                'type': int(b[1]),\n                'pd': float(b[2]),\n                'qd': float(b[3]),\n                'gs': float(b[4]),\n                'bs': float(b[5]),\n                'vm_init': float(b[6]) if b[6] != 0 else 1.0,\n                'va_init': float(b[7]) * math.pi / 180.0 if b[7] != 0 else 0.0,\n                'vmin': float(b[11]),\n                'vmax': float(b[10])\n            }\n\n        gen_data = {}\n        gen_cost = {}\n        for i, g in enumerate(self.generators):\n            bus_id = int(g[0])\n            if bus_id not in gen_data:\n                gen_data[bus_id] = []\n            gen_data[bus_id].append({\n                'gen_idx': i,\n                'pg': float(g[1]),\n                'qg': float(g[2]),\n                'qmax': float(g[3]),\n                'qmin': float(g[4]),\n                'vg': float(g[5]) if g[5] != 0 else 1.0,\n                'pmax': float(g[8]),\n                'pmin': float(g[9])\n            })\n\n        for i, gc in enumerate(self.gencost):\n            model = int(gc[0])\n            if model == 2:\n                c2 = float(gc[4])\n                c1 = float(gc[5])\n                c0 = float(gc[6])\n                gen_cost[i] = {'c2': c2, 'c1': c1, 'c0': c0}\n\n        ref_bus = None\n        for bus_id, data in bus_data.items():\n            if data['type'] == 3:\n                ref_bus = bus_id\n                break\n        if ref_bus is None:\n            ref_bus = 1\n\n        print(f\"Reference bus: {ref_bus}\")\n\n        total_load = sum(b['pd'] for b in bus_data.values())\n        print(f\"Total load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n\n        V = np.ones(self.n_bus, dtype=complex)\n        V[ref_bus-1] = bus_data[ref_bus]['vm_init'] * complex(1.0, 0.0)\n\n        for i, (bus_id, data) in enumerate(bus_data.items()):\n            if i != ref_bus - 1 and abs(data['vm_init'] - 1.0) > 1e-6:\n                V[i] = data['vm_init'] * complex(math.cos(data['va_init']), math.sin(data['va_init']))\n\n        P_gen = np.zeros(self.n_bus)\n        Q_gen = np.zeros(self.n_bus)\n\n        for bus_id, gens in gen_data.items():\n            idx = bus_id - 1\n            for gen in gens:\n                P_gen[idx] += gen['pg']\n                Q_gen[idx] += gen['qg']\n\n        print(f\"Initial total generation: {np.sum(P_gen):.2f} MW\")\n\n        V = self.solve_power_flow(Y, bus_data, gen_data, ref_bus, P_gen, Q_gen)\n\n        V = self.optimize_dispatch(Y, bus_data, gen_data, gen_cost, ref_bus, V)\n\n        results = self.extract_results(V, Y, bus_data, gen_data, gen_cost, ref_bus)\n        return results\n\n    def solve_power_flow(self, Y, bus_data, gen_data, ref_bus, P_gen_init, Q_gen_init):\n        \"\"\"Solve power flow with fixed generation\"\"\"\n        V = np.ones(self.n_bus, dtype=complex)\n        V[ref_bus-1] = bus_data[ref_bus]['vm_init'] * complex(1.0, 0.0)\n\n        P_gen = P_gen_init.copy()\n        Q_gen = Q_gen_init.copy()\n\n        max_iter = 100\n        tolerance = 1e-6\n\n        for iteration in range(max_iter):\n            S_injected = V * np.conj(Y @ V)\n            P_calc = np.real(S_injected)\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_injected)\n\n            P_mismatch = np.zeros(self.n_bus)\n            Q_mismatch = np.zeros(self.n_bus)\n\n            for bus_id, data in bus_data.items():\n                idx = bus_id - 1\n                pd = data['pd']\n                qd = data['qd']\n                P_mismatch[idx] = P_gen[idx] - pd - P_calc[idx]\n                Q_mismatch[idx] = Q_gen[idx] - qd - Q_calc[idx]\n\n            P_mismatch[ref_bus-1] = 0\n            Q_mismatch[ref_bus-1] = 0\n\n            max_mismatch = max(np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch)), np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch)))\n            if max_mismatch < tolerance:\n                print(f\"Power flow converged in {iteration} iterations, max mismatch: {max_mismatch:.6e}\")\n                break\n\n            J = self.build_jacobian_pf(V, Y, bus_data, P_gen, Q_gen)\n            mismatch = np.concatenate([P_mismatch, Q_mismatch])\n\n            try:\n                delta = np.linalg.solve(J, mismatch)\n            except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n                delta = np.linalg.pinv(J) @ mismatch\n\n            dVa = delta[:self.n_bus]\n            dVm = delta[self.n_bus:]\n\n            V = V * (1 + dVm) * complex(np.cos(dVa), np.sin(dVa))\n\n            for bus_id, data in bus_data.items():\n                idx = bus_id - 1\n                vm = abs(V[idx])\n                if vm < data['vmin']:\n                    V[idx] = data['vmin'] * complex(math.cos(np.angle(V[idx])), math.sin(np.angle(V[idx])))\n                elif vm > data['vmax']:\n                    V[idx] = data['vmax'] * complex(math.cos(np.angle(V[idx])), math.sin(np.angle(V[idx])))\n\n            if iteration % 20 == 0:\n                print(f\"PF Iteration {iteration}: max mismatch = {max_mismatch:.6e}\")\n\n        else:\n            print(f\"Power flow did not fully converge, final max mismatch: {max_mismatch:.6e}\")\n\n        return V\n\n    def build_jacobian_pf(self, V, Y, bus_data, P_gen, Q_gen):\n        \"\"\"Build power flow Jacobian\"\"\"\n        n = len(V)\n        J = np.zeros((2*n, 2*n))\n\n        H = np.zeros((n, n))\n        N = np.zeros((n, n))\n        M = np.zeros((n, n))\n        L = np.zeros((n, n))\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            Vi = V[i]\n            for j in range(n):\n                if i != j:\n                    Vj = V[j]\n                    Yij = Y[i, j]\n\n                    H[i, j] = abs(Vi)**2 * np.real(Yij) - np.real(Vi * np.conj(Yij) * Vj)\n                    N[i, j] = -abs(Vi)**2 * np.imag(Yij) - np.imag(Vi * np.conj(Yij) * Vj)\n                    M[i, j] = abs(Vi)**2 * np.imag(Yij) - np.imag(Vi * np.conj(Yij) * Vj)\n                    L[i, j] = abs(Vi)**2 * np.real(Yij) + np.real(Vi * np.conj(Yij) * Vj)\n\n            H[i, i] = -np.sum([abs(Vj)**2 * np.real(Yij) for j, Vj in enumerate(V) if i != j])\n            N[i, i] = -np.sum([abs(Vj)**2 * np.imag(Yij) for j, Vj in enumerate(V) if i != j])\n            M[i, i] = -np.sum([-abs(Vj)**2 * np.imag(Yij) for j, Vj in enumerate(V) if i != j])\n            L[i, i] = -np.sum([abs(Vj)**2 * np.real(Yij) for j, Vj in enumerate(V) if i != j])\n\n        J[:n, :n] = H\n        J[:n, n:] = N\n        J[n:, :n] = M\n        J[n:, n:] = L\n\n        return J\n\n    def optimize_dispatch(self, Y, bus_data, gen_data, gen_cost, ref_bus, V_init):\n        \"\"\"Optimize generator dispatch using simplified approach\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nOptimizing generator dispatch...\")\n\n        all_gens = []\n        for dVa = delta[:self.n_bus]\n            dVm = delta[self.n_bus:]\n\n            V = V * (1 + dVm) * np.exp(1j * dVa)\n\n            if iteration % 10 == 0:\n                print(f\"Iteration gen_data):\n        n = len(V)\n        J = np.zeros((2*n, 2*n))\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            for j in range(n):\n                if i != j:\n                    Vi = V[i]\n                    Vj = V[j]\n                    Yij = Y[i, j]\n\n                    H_ij = abs(Vi)**2 * np.real(Yij) - np.real(Vi * np.conj(Yij) * Vj)\n                    N_ij = -abs(Vi)**2 * np.imag(Yij) - np.imag(Vi * np.conj(Yij) * Vj)\n                    M_ij = abs(Vi)**2 * np.imag(Yij) - np.imag(Vi * np.conj(Yij) * Vj)\n                    L_ij = abs(Vi)**2 * np.real(Yij) + np.real(Vi * np.conj(Yij) * Vj)\n\n                    J[i, j] = H_ij\n                    J[i, n+j] = N_ij\n                    J[n+i, j] = M_ij\n                    J[n+i, n+j] = L_ij\n\n            H_ii = 0\n   L_ii += abs(Vj)**2 * np.real(Yij)\n\n            J[i, i] = -H_ii\n            J[i, n+i] = -N_ii\n            J[n+i, i] = -M_ii\n            J[n+i, n+i] = -L_ii\n\n        return J\n\n    def extract_results(self, V, Y, bus_data, gen_data, gen_cost, ref_bus):\n        results = {\n            'summary': {},\n            'generators': [],\n            'buses': [],\n + c1 * Pg + c0\n\n            total_gen_MW += Pg\n            total_gen_MVAr += Qg\n\n            results['generators'].append({\n                'id': gen_idx + 1,\n                'bus': bus_id,\n 'qmax_MVAr': gen['qmax']\n            })\n\n        branch_flows = []\n        for branch in self.branches:\n            fbus = int(branch[0]) - 1\n            tbus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            rateA = float(branch[5]) if branch[5] > 0 else 0\n\n            Sf = V[fbus] * np.conj(Y[fbus, fbus] * V[fbus] + Y[fbus, tbus] * V[tbus])\n            St = V[tbus] * np.conj(Y[tbus, fbus] * V[tbus] + self.baseMVA\n            })\n\n        branch_flows.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n        results['most_loaded_branches'] = branch_flows[:10]\n\n        total_losses_MW =            Y[tbus, fbus] -= y / np.conj(a)\n            else:\n                Y[fbus, fbus] += y + Y_shunt\n                Y[tbus, tbus] += y + Y_shunt\n                Y[fbus, tbus] -= y\n                Y[tbus, fbus] -= y\n\n        return Y\n\n    def solve(self):\n        Y = self.build_Y_matrix()\n\n        bus_data = {}\n        for b in self.buses:\n            bus_id = int(b[0])\n dtype=complex)\n        V[ref_bus-1] = bus_data[ref_bus]['vm_init'] * (1 + 0j)\n\n        max_iter = 100\n        tolerance = 1e-6\n        damping = 0.5\n\n        for iteration in range(max_iter):\n            S_injected = V * np.conj(Y @ V)\n            P_calc = np.real(S_injected)\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_injected)\n\n            P_mismatch = np.zeros(self.n_bus)\n            Q_mismatch = np.zeros(self.n_bus)\n\n            for bus_id, data in bus_data.items():\n                idx = bus_id - 1\n                pd = data['pd']\n                qd = data['qd']\n\n                pg = 0\n                qg = 0\n                if bus_id in gen_data:\n                    for gen in gen_data[bus_id]:\n                        pg += gen['pg']\n                        qg += gen['qg']\n\n                P_mismatch[idx] = pg - pd - P_calc[idx]\n                Q_mismatch[idx] = qg - qd - Q_calc[idx]\n\n            P_mismatch[ref_bus-1] = 0\n            Q_mismatch[ref_bus-1] = 0\n\n            max_mismatch = max(np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch)), np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch)))\n            if max_mismatch < tolerance:\n                print(f\"Converged in           tap = 1.0\n\n            z = r + 1j * x\n            y = 1.0 / z\n            Y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n            if abs(tap - 1.0) > 1e-6:\n                a = tap * polar_to_complex(1, fbus] -= y / np.conj(a)\n            else:\n                Y[fbus, fbus] += y + Y_shunt\n                Y[tbus, tbus] += y + Y_shunt\n                Y[fbus, tbus] -= y\n                Y[tbus, fbus] -= y\n\n        return Y\n\n    def solve(self):\n        Y = self.build_Y_matrix()\n\n        bus_data = {}\n        for b in self.buses:\n            bus_id = int(b[0])\n dtype=complex)\n\n        for bus_id, data in bus_data.items():\n            idx = bus_id - 1\n            vm = data['vm_init'] if data['vm_init'] > 0 else 1.0\n            va = data['va_init']\n        Q_mismatch = np.zeros(self.n_bus)\n\n            for bus_id, data in bus_data.items():\n                idx = bus_id - 1\n                pd = data['pd']\n                qd = data['qd']\n\n Q_mismatch[ref_bus-1] = 0\n\n            max_mismatch = max(np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch)), np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch)))\n            if max_mismatch < tolerance:\n                print(f\"Converged in   return V\n\n    def build_jacobian_simple(self, V, Y, bus_data, gen_data):\n        n = len(V)\n        J = np.zeros((2*n, 2*n))\n\n        G = np.real(Y)\n        B = np.imag(Y)\n\n        for i in   H_ij = -vm_i * vm_j * (G_ij * math.sin(va_diff) - B_ij * math.cos(va_diff))\n                    N_ij = -vm_i * vm_j * (G_ij * math.cos(va_diff) + B_ij * math.sin(va_diff))\n                    M_ij = vm_i * vm_j * (G_ij * math.cos(va_diff) + B_ij * math.sin(va_diff))\n                    L_ij = -vm_i * vm_j * (G_ij * math.sin(va_diff) - B_ij * math.cos(va_diff))\n\n                    J[i, j] = G_ij = G[i, j]\n                    B_ij = B[i, j]\n\n                    H_ii += vm_i * vm_j * (G_ij * math.sin(va_diff) - B_ij * math.cos(va_diff))\n                    N_ii += vm_i * vm_j * (-G_ij vm_i\n\n        return J\n\n    def extract_results(self, V, Y, bus_data, gen_data, gen_cost, ref_bus):\n        results = {\n            'summary': {},\n            'generators': [],\n self.n_bus\n        B = np.zeros((n-1, n-1))\n        Pbus = np.zeros(n)\n\n        bus_map = {}\n        idx = 0\n        for bus_id in sorted(bus_data.keys()):\n            if bus_id != ref_bus:\n })\n\n        branch_flows = []\n        total_losses_MW = 0\n\n        for branch in self.branches:\n            fbus = int(branch[0]) - 1\n            tbus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            r = power flow\"\"\"\n        n = self.n_bus\n        B = np.zeros((n-1, n-1))\n        Pbus = np.zeros(n)\n\n        bus_map = {}\n        idx = 0\n        for bus_id in sorted(bus_data.keys()):\n             j = bus_map[tbus]\n                B[i, i] += 1.0 / x\n                B[j, j] += 1.0 / x\n                B[i, j] -= 1.0 / x\n                B[j, i] -= 1.0 / x\n            elif fbus !=
